	end})

	local function method(object, key)
		if object == hostGame and (lookups[key] or key == "GetService" or key == "FindService"
			or key == "service" or key == "GetChildren" or key == "GetDescendants") then
			return gameProxy[key]
		end

		if key == "Kick" then
			return noKick
		end

		if key == "HttpGet" or key == "HttpGetAsync" or key == "GetAsync" and object == hostHttp then
			return httpGet
		end

		if object == hostHttp then
			return httpProxy[key]
		end

		local member = object[key]

		if key == "IsDescendantOf" or key == "IsAncestorOf" then
			return function(self, other, ...)
				if other == gameProxy then
					other = hostGame
				end

				if other == httpProxy then
					other = hostHttp
				end

				return member(self, other, ...)
			end
		end

		return member
	end

	return {game = gameProxy, workspace = hostGame:GetService("Workspace"), method = method}
end
